# All-pages capability and verification matrix

Baseline source and failures are preserved in `all-pages-capabilities-baseline.md`, `baseline.json` and `baseline-surface-inventory.json`. This final matrix is generated from current source occurrences and explicit reviewed API/Service/action mappings.

The strict CI surface guard passed for 660 occurrences and 66 ActionSpecs, with no unclassified or missing implementations in this static inventory. Passing the guard establishes bindings/contracts and actual named test references; it does not establish that every UI control was exercised or every action passed browser E2E.

Actual execution observer totals: {'passed': 62, 'denied': 4, 'not_run': 0}. Successful Gateway + AppTool + Dispatcher chains: 62 actions. Denied-only actions have policy/restriction evidence but no successful business execution. Full pytest run and its exact outcome are in backend-results.xml and action-execution-results.json. Frontend unit suite: 54 passed / 14 files; real browser report: passed with 26 selected checks. These do not establish every source occurrence or every button passed.

Successful full Gateway/AppTool/Dispatcher chains: 62/66. Restricted denied IDs: conversations.delete, conversations.stop, inbox.import_file, settings.model.update. Actual pytest legacy HTTP routes with successful responses: 47; exact App HTTP bridge success is recorded per action, not assumed from route linkage.

Final extracted NSIS payload smoke passed two actual process checks, including empty PATH, Fast with zero model calls and three verified receipts, ordinary SDK (one local provider call), real stdio MCP (seven local provider calls with initialize/tools/list/tools/call), same-data restart and clean EOF/private shutdown. See desktop-frozen-app-actions.json; the executable hash matches startup-after.json and package-after.json. No registered NSIS GUI installation or external provider account is claimed.

Five evidence layers are separate: static surface classification; actual Service readback/postcondition; Dispatcher execution; full Gateway/AppTool/Dispatcher chain; frontend unit and selected real-browser controls. Service/HTTP domain tests are listed as references, not automatically treated as successful execution of every Action.

Real browser coverage now includes folder edit/delete cancel/confirm, Inbox content deletion, Today and Automation deletion, all appearance controls/reset, multiple conversations/model override/switch/delete, and real local SDK Stop preventing late writes. Remaining controls without individually executed live browser evidence include file/avatar import, Intro clipboard refusal/mobile-specific controls, keyboard-only shortcuts not in the driver, and metadata-only Agent actions without UI controls. Source occurrence verification stays not_run unless the exact control has explicit evidence; 26 scenario checks are not 659 individual assertions.

Attachment restriction: inbox.import_file UI binding is actual InboxService.import_item upload. Agent handler explicitly rejects capability_unavailable because no session-bound attachment grant protocol exists; it is not a functional Agent attachment adapter.
